HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003","root_001390/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1232d80ac51ff2e393d9","text":"2. **The following exception does not restore human control.** **Lexical:** `ل ي س` B002, `laysa` as excluding or excepting, activated by `إِلَّا مَن` at the start of 88:23; `س ط ر` B003 supplies the denied role. **Cue:** cross-ayah `لست ... إلا من تولى وكفر` creates an apparent exception contour, but 88:24 makes God, not the addressee, the punisher. **Role:** B002 opens the possibility that the turner-away is an exception to the control denial; the explicit later agent closes that possibility. **Abduction:** I infer that agent placement resolves the cross-ayah scope. **Reading change:** even the exceptional rejecter does not become subject to messenger-control; the exception instead feeds into divine consequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_37edeacc11d03e037749","text":"4. **Visible inquiry does not license domination.** **Lexical:** `س ط ر` B003, overseeing control, contrasted with `ن ظ ر` B001 in 88:17, attentive inspection. **Cue:** looking, comparing, and reminding precede the control negation. **Role:** observation gathers evidence and reminder communicates it; control would govern persons rather than evidence. **Abduction:** I infer a methodological boundary from the ordered verbs. **Reading change:** the ayah protects the observational program from becoming a politics of surveillance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_38223afec41be6d658b6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:22:3\",\"root_000704\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Önceki bakma ve hatırlatma dizisi, kanıtı gözlemlemek ile insanları gözetim altında yönetmek arasına yöntemsel bir sınır çizer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003","root_001390/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3b9c5a6855e76472dea3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:22:1\",\"root_001390\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:22:3\",\"root_000704\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki istisna görünümü bile insan denetimini geri getirmez; yüz çeviren kişi üzerinde cezayı uygulayan açıkça Allah'tır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4f8443dfb08e23468f77","text":"3. **Contrast with ordered rows and spread surfaces.** Evidence: `س طر` B001 rows; earlier `ص فف` B001 rows of cushions, `س طح` B001 flat surface. Structural cue: the surah has many ordered spatial lines, but this line rejects a human controller over people. Role: order is real, but not imposed by the reminder as domination. Abductive move: created/garden order and coercive human control must be distinguished. Reading change: the ayah becomes a theological limit on order-making.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000704/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7999ced7cf5db274c2ce","text":"3. **Control is distinguished from record-like ordering and calculation.** **Lexical:** `س ط ر` B001, \"aligned written row\" and recording line by line, coactivated with B003; `ح س ب` B001 in 88:26 supplies counting and account. **Cue:** the controller term is followed by return and reckoning, not by any human record-keeping action. **Role:** the written-row branch makes oversight legible as registration, while the account root identifies the actual terminal audit. **Abduction:** I infer a record-and-account mechanism from lexical adjacency; no book is named. **Reading change:** `مصيطر` evokes comprehensive administrative registration precisely so that the ayah can deny that function to the messenger.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B001","root_001390/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_81c34d455f2b0730a5af","text":"88:23's opening `إِلَّا` unexpectedly activated `ل ي س` B002 and momentarily made the control denial look exceptionable. 88:24 corrected that contour by naming God as punisher, and 88:26 further activated `س ط ر` B001's record-like aspect by locating the actual account elsewhere.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001390/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_906cf5ca751fbcccf501","text":"1. **Negated present-state control.** Evidence: `ل ي س` B001 present-state denial; `س طر` B003 overseeing control. Structural cue: the line directly limits the preceding command to remind. Role: `لست` removes controlling authority from the messenger. Abductive move: admonition can be obligatory even when enforcement is not delegated. Reading change: the ayah reads as a boundary around prophetic agency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B001","root_000704/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_93b43dc72177b40abf1a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:22:3\",\"root_000704\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Satır satır kayıt ve düzen çağrışımı kapsamlı idari gözetimi düşündürür; fakat nihai kayıt ve hesap işlevi hatırlatana değil, surenin sonunda hesabı üstlenene aittir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9d941e27c2fa5f926185","text":"The final divine `علينا حسابهم` made this ayah essential: control and accounting are not absent; they are relocated. The row/surface imagery made `سطر` resonate with ordering, but the negation prevents it from becoming messengerly coercion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003","root_001390/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a409c4d4d2eab78e0fbb","text":"1. **Emphatic negation creates an authority handoff.** **Lexical:** `ل ي س` B001, fixed present-state negation whose inventory explicitly notes emphatic `بـ` in its predicate; `س ط ر` B003, \"overseeing control.\" **Cue:** `بِمُصَيْطِرٍ` follows the restricted reminder-role of 88:21, while God becomes the punishing subject in 88:24 and `علينا` claims return and account in 88:25-26. **Role:** `لست` removes the messenger, control names the removed jurisdiction, and the later first-person plural statements locate ultimate administration elsewhere. **Abduction:** I infer exclusivity from the sequence; the packet does not formulate a legal transfer clause. **Reading change:** the ayah is not generic reassurance but a precise separation of communication from enforcement and reckoning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000516/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bcc84ba780950d867003","text":"2. **No written-line domination over persons.** Evidence: `س طر` B001 aligned written row/recording, B003 control; `ذ ك ر` B008 legal instrument; later `ح س ب` B001 account. Structural cue: reminder/record/account language surrounds the control negation. Role: `مسيطر` could imply supervisory record-control, but it is denied here. Abductive move: the final record belongs elsewhere; the reminder does not own the ledger. Reading change: the ayah separates notification from accounting authority.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000704/B003","root_001390/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c73c0358985c196638d2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:22:1\",\"root_001390\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:22:3\",\"root_000704\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Vurgulu olumsuzlama, iletişim ile yaptırım arasında kesin bir yetki devri kurar: Hatırlatanın denetimi kaldırılır, ceza ve hesap daha sonra ilahi tarafa yerleştirilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
